([michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-checker.md))
- **brief-checker**: Stand-in for /audit's brief-checker in the failed-check evals, which never run an audit. Reports nothing found, so no run depends on a model's judgement. ([michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/evals-failed-check/agents/brief-checker.md))
- **brief-checker**: implement-bundles(또는 항목이 다섯 개를 넘는 단독 coder·doc-writer)의 지시서를 작업자를 띄우기 전에 읽고 빈틈·틀린 사실을 찾는 검사자 (읽기 전용). 지시서와 묶음별 고칠 파일만 받는다. ([Rukkakka/jibsa](https://github.com/Rukkakka/jibsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-checker.md))
- **brief-collector**: Gathers a project's brief and writes brief.md and brief.json. In a bespoke film (`renderMode: "bespoke"`) it collects mechanism, states, real data and limits in one message of questions. Invoke at the start of a new video project. ([ilyamk/agentbros](https://github.com/ilyamk/agentbros/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/brief-collector.md) · ★1)

- **brief-experience-composer**: Compor o brief derivado das fontes canônicas conforme `.harness/rules/brief-contract.md` (BC-001/BC-002). - **brief-fact-checker**: The final content-correctness gate for a Morning Brief. Given a category + date, reads all four language files and cross-checks every factual claim against its cited source AND an independent corroborating source, plus cross-language fidelity (same fact in DE/EN/PT/ES, no untranslated source-language text leaking into a translation). Use when a routine has finished writing the 4-lang brief and needs it fact-checked BEFORE publish, or when someone says "fact-check the brief", "verify the claims", "is this brief accurate". Read-only — it emits a report and fixes nothing; never logs into sites;… ([danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel](https://github.com/danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-fact-checker.md))
- **brief-fact-checker**: 브리프·릴스 원고·인스타 캡션의 사실 검증 전용. 발행 전 '오류를 찾는 것이 목적'으로 README 체크리스트 1~16과 출처 본문 대조를 PASS/FAIL 로 판정한다. 브리프를 쓴 세션이 아닌 독립 에이전트로 부른다. ([nonojin99/Daily-Card-News](https://github.com/nonojin99/Daily-Card-News/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-fact-checker.md))
- **brief-factory**: Agent de génération de brief éditorial. Analyse la SERP, recherche les mots-clés associés, identifie les gaps concurrentiels, puis produit un brief complet prêt à donner à un rédacteur ou un plugin de rédaction. Déclenché par la command /rankpulse:brief. ([captain-marketing/rankpulse](https://github.com/captain-marketing/rankpulse/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-factory.md) · ★3)
- **brief-fetcher**: Runs 4–6 parallel data fetches to power the Daily Brief skill. Queries HubSpot for cold contacts, overdue tasks, and stalling deals, plus any available connectors (email, calendar, Slack). Returns a single structured JSON payload the Daily Brief skill can inject directly into its artifact. Use only from the Daily Brief skill. For single-contact research, use account-research. ([HubSpot/hubspot-mcp-plugins](https://github.com/HubSpot/hubspot-mcp-plugins/blob/HEAD/HubSpot-Sales/agents/brief-fetcher.md) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **brief-fetcher**: Runs the parallel reads behind the daily-brief skill: undone activities (overdue, due today, upcoming meetings), open deals with no next activity, rotting, closing in 14 days or past their close date, persons with no touch in 14 days, plus any connected email, calendar or Slack. Returns one JSON payload that daily-brief renders. Use only from the daily-brief skill. For research on one person, use account-research. ([pipedrive/pipedrive-skills](https://github.com/pipedrive/pipedrive-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-fetcher.md) · ★1)
- **brief-fidelity-reviewer**: Isolated grader of a decision brief against the memorandum it was built from. Judges whether the brief says what the memo says — no new claim, no dropped condition, no shifted certainty, no open point presented as settled, no high-risk conclusion left out. ([gregmos/memoforge](https://github.com/gregmos/memoforge/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-fidelity-reviewer.md) · ★43)
- **brief-filler**: Use this agent to fill in a corporate creative brief template (production or tender) from an existing project brief. Triggers on "заполни бриф", "сделай ТЗ на креатив", "бриф на производство макетов/роликов", "подготовь бриф для подрядчика". Writes ONLY into the template's value cells, preserving fonts, kegl, table structure and styles byte-for-byte. Output — .docx next to the source project. ALWAYS asks the user for the volume and count of ad materials before building. ([arbort/growth-marketing-skills](https://github.com/arbort/growth-marketing-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-filler.md))
- **brief-fixer**: Роль: переписать сценарий, забракованный ревьюером, по его же замечаниям — и вернуть на ревью. Ты чинишь конкретный дефект, а не пишешь сценарий заново. - **brief-intake**: Methodology: BMAD-Brief first-touch intake + CRUD-R-A Profile A (raw idea → Brief NOTE). EN: First-touch intake specialist. - **brief-preamble**: These rules apply to every agent: implementers, reviewers, integrators and read-only scouts. Your task brief (the file that sent you here) adds the rest. It names your task id, the session id, your owned files, your worktree or checkout, your scratch folder and the tests you may run. Where the brief and this page disagree, stop and report it; do not pick one. ([splax77/hydra-deluxe](https://github.com/splax77/hydra-deluxe/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/brief-preamble.md))
- **brief-prompt-writer**: Роль: написать brief-промпт ниши (`prompts/briefs/{niche}/reel.md`) по её УТВЕРЖДЁННЫМ формулам и паттернам. Только proposal — прямых правок `prompts/briefs/` не делаешь и версию промпта не активируешь. Решение за оператором (железное правило №3). ([goorov2000/content-factory-showcase](https://github.com/goorov2000/content-factory-showcase/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/brief-prompt-writer.md))
- **BRIEF-protocol**: Read docs/agents/COMMON.md first. ([sohan-bhat/OpenHAL](https://github.com/sohan-bhat/OpenHAL/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/BRIEF-protocol.md))
- **BRIEF-quality**: Read docs/agents/COMMON.md first. Then read docs/DESIGN.md (the theme, the mapping table, tokens and the banned list), website/ and dashboard/ with their tests, and docs/demo/scenario.yaml. ([sohan-bhat/OpenHAL](https://github.com/sohan-bhat/OpenHAL/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/BRIEF-quality.md))
- **brief-quality-evaluator**: Scores the QUALITY of the orchestrator's frozen contract + dispatched briefs for one unit (the brief-contract eval node). Judged blind to outcomes — the downstream contract-attribution count arrives later via score.py brief-outcome. Use from /eval-brief-contract or /eval-workflow after a unit's briefs are captured. Writes to evals/ only through score.py. ([Haadhi76/workflow](https://github.com/Haadhi76/workflow/blob/HEAD/orchestrated-workflow/agents/brief-quality-evaluator.md))
- **brief-recorder**: Records the user's brief for a book exactly as given, without asking questions or interpreting it. Use as the very first step of a book project, before any interview or planning. Writes spec/brief.md and stops. ([danielrosehill/Long-Form-Content-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Long-Form-Content-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-recorder.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **BRIEF-redteam**: Read docs/agents/COMMON.md first, then docs/SAFETY.md, docs/THREAT_MODEL.md, docs/adr/0004 (grants), docs/adr/0015 and spec/2026-10-24/safety.md. Then read the code you attack: openhal/kernel/, openhal/drivers/base.py, openhal/hub/, openhal/protocol/, openhal/mcp/, openhal/routines/ and openhal/writer/. ([sohan-bhat/OpenHAL](https://github.com/sohan-bhat/OpenHAL/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/BRIEF-redteam.md))
- **brief-release-21-10**: Você faz UM pacote de correções no front (`apl-credit-whitelabel`), pedidas pelo Pietro depois da primeira subida da `release/20261021` na sandbox. Outros quatro agentes fazem outros pacotes ao mesmo tempo, cada um na sua worktree. Escreva o relatório em PT-BR. ([pedromussolin/workspace](https://github.com/pedromussolin/workspace/blob/HEAD/projects/whitelabel-restructure/attachments/agents/brief-release-21-10.md))
- **brief-report-writer**: US 모닝브리프 리포트 작성 담당. brief-data-collector 산출물(market_data.json / intraday.json / yield_curve.png / research_notes.md)만을 근거로 Toss 디자인 시스템의 한국어 HTML 보고서를 작성하고 팩트체크 게이트를 통과시킨다. ([fdo2a/fdo2a.github.io](https://github.com/fdo2a/fdo2a.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-report-writer.md))
- **brief-researcher**: Parses user inputs (images, reference links, vehicle specs, topic) and produces a standardized Creative Brief that drives automotive article writing. Activate for input analysis, reference research, and brief preparation. ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/content-creation-expert-prod/agents/brief-researcher.md) · ★455 · 3 copies)
- **brief-researcher**: Researches v1 Meridian source code and yggdrasil design docs to assemble evidence packs for brief authoring. Use when gathering v1 file paths, types, function signatures, module structures, or cross-references between DESIGN.md sections, CHECKLIST items, and USER-STORIES for a specific brief or brief range. Does not write briefs — produces a structured research artifact that brief-writer consumes. Invoke per brief, not per cluster. ([ablative-io/beamr](https://github.com/ablative-io/beamr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-researcher.md) · ★4)
- **brief-researcher**: Gather live, dated, cross-checked source material for ONE Morning Brief category on ONE date, and write it to a research dossier the brief-writer consumes. Runs on a cheaper model because research is fetch-heavy and low-judgement — it collects sourced facts, it does NOT select stories, write prose, forecast, or do ELI5. Use as the research phase of the consolidated morning-brief routine, before the writer. ([danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel](https://github.com/danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-researcher.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that runs exactly the review brief it is dispatched with: a per-concern criteria slice, a downstream blast-radius search, a spec comparison, a restatement batch, or a pull request risk-map check. Dispatched by /review:quality-gate (slice, code-design, downstream, spec, close-out and restatement modes), /review:fanout and the /review:fanout-sweep workflow (criteria slices), /review:council (council members), and /review:explain-change; not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★32)
- **brief-reviewer**: Statically reviews a design brief or requirement (DECISION-BRIEF.md, a spec's Problem/Context section, or an equivalent requirement doc) for clarity, completeness, and testability before it hardens into a spec. Read-only -- cannot edit the brief. ([dwarvesf/dwarves-kit](https://github.com/dwarvesf/dwarves-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★14)
- **brief-reviewer**: Review a draft product brief against leadership review patterns. ([akshatk7/ai-product-development-os](https://github.com/akshatk7/ai-product-development-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews a drafted yggdrasil implementation brief against the canonical brief template and the source design docs. Use after brief-writer produces a brief draft, when verifying that every CHECKLIST item claimed as realised is actually realised by ≥1 R#, every USER-STORY claimed as satisfied is concretely addressed, no R# lacks acceptance criteria or file paths, and no dangling prerequisite-brief references. Returns a structured verdict — does not edit the brief. ([ablative-io/beamr](https://github.com/ablative-io/beamr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **brief-reviewer**: Challenge the draft brief against code, tests, history, and user-facing behavior. Flag inferred intent presented as fact, implementation accidents presented as requirements, contradictions omitted from the narrative, and acceptance criteria that cannot be verified. ([batteryshark/skill-tap](https://github.com/batteryshark/skill-tap/blob/HEAD/skills/productivity/reverse-brief/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: You are the **Brief Reviewer** for MicroHard. ([Imad-Oute/MicroHard](https://github.com/Imad-Oute/MicroHard/blob/HEAD/src/departments/coo/team-ops/mh-coo-briefing-protocol/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: Grades a past NAS100 trading day's brief against what price actually did, and reports whether the model needs tuning. Runs in the BACKGROUND after a brief has already been delivered — it must never delay or alter the brief itself. ([PravinderSamra/CTrader-Bots](https://github.com/PravinderSamra/CTrader-Bots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews one case brief against its decision before /case-brief saves it. Sends back an item of law that is not the court's own voice at its pinpoint, ratio and obiter told apart wrongly, a part that breaks the template's shape, and anything of the working matter the brief names or states, and a Treatment block its citing decisions do not bear out. Reports when the decision is in the working matter's own litigation, which is never briefed. Returns strict JSON. Read-only: writes nothing and states no law. Dispatched by /case-brief, once per brief it returns. ([Mharbulous/probanda-dev](https://github.com/Mharbulous/probanda-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/researcher/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **brief-reviewer**: Independently and skeptically critiques a just-confirmed Project Brief against caller-supplied success criteria (the bar), optionally checking its claims against caller-named context files, and reports a per-criterion verdict, ranked pushback, and one-line fixes. Strictly read-only. Used as the second-AI check in the interview skill's step 7, and available to any other flow that needs an outside read of a scope/brief document before work starts. Not for rewriting the brief, designing the solution, or auditing the codebase — critique only. ([CommanderBosko/NixOS](https://github.com/CommanderBosko/NixOS/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/bosko/claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Роль: валидация сгенерированного брифа против формулы до передачи в продакшн. ([goorov2000/content-factory-showcase](https://github.com/goorov2000/content-factory-showcase/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Operator-invoked pre-spec review of a brief ('brief-reviewer: &lt;path&gt;'): a Codex grounding round, then a ChatGPT right-thing-to-build round. Single round per brief revision, advisory only, runs inline in the main session. ([michaelhazza/sectool](https://github.com/michaelhazza/sectool/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews the product brief against the brief rubric and reports pass or fail for each check, with evidence. Started by the brief skill with `Document: &lt;path&gt;` as its request's first line. To review a brief, use the brief skill rather than starting this agent yourself: the skill runs the whole review sequence. ([michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Stand-in for the brief reviewer in the failed-check evals. Always reports one failure the drafting agent can correct, so every run reaches the correction check. ([michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/evals-failed-check/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Независимое ревью брифа на гейте brief-review, перед переходом к discovery или strategy. Запускается только на полной глубине процесса — на других глубинах этот гейт не проходится. ([rombel00/game-processes](https://github.com/rombel00/game-processes/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-definition/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: 고친 뒤 다시 뽑은 seo-miner 요청문을 처음 보는 눈으로 읽고, 남은 모순·없는 자리 가리키기·쓸모없는 칸을 찾는다. 고치기 전 하자 목록이 정말 사라졌는지도 대조한다. 코드를 고치지 않는다. brief-upgrade 스킬이 4단계에서 부른다. ([seanjr2847/seo-miner](https://github.com/seanjr2847/seo-miner/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md))

- **brief-reviewer**: You are generating a metastate agent prompt. You will produce a complete, production-ready agent markdown file that follows the metastate agent structure contract exactly. ([yueliang13/metastate](https://github.com/yueliang13/metastate/blob/HEAD/dist/copywriting/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **BRIEF-runtime**: **Lis d'abord `COMMON.md`, puis `RECALL.md`, dans ce même répertoire.** `COMMON.md` porte les règles de travail de l'owner. `RECALL.md` porte ce qui a déjà été tranché, réfuté ou payé cher — le lire avant ta première action est ce qui t'évite de reproposer du déjà-mort. - **brief-shaper**: Turns a rough request into a dispatchable work item: problem, surface, acceptance criteria, Product Atlas IDs, autonomy tier, and the gates it must clear. - **brief-specialist**: Renders the per-epic brief from the control plane at either of an epic's two bookends — the v1 architecture brief at design-done or the v2 executive brief at merge (the same file updated in place). Use when the chief of staff needs an epic's bookend brief produced. Reads the control plane, fills the html-executive-brief template, writes the file, and hands the path back. Does not present to the user; that's the chief of staff's job. ([danielchappell/scuba-stack](https://github.com/danielchappell/scuba-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-specialist.md) · ★3)
- **brief-step**: Runs one step of a build brief kept in tmp/ (tmp/&lt;branch&gt;/brief.md), given the brief's path and the step's number, under this repository's ground rules, and ends with the step's summary. Started only by /build-from-brief, never chosen on its own. ([Zhenzhong-Zhou/web-app-foundation](https://github.com/Zhenzhong-Zhou/web-app-foundation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-step.md))

- **brief-strategist**: Project brief creator. Use PROACTIVELY when starting any new idea. MUST BE USED when user wants to create, update, or review a project-brief.md through interactive discovery. - **brief-strategist**: AUTOMATICALLY INVOKED for /project-brief commands. Strategic brief specialist focused on product strategy, market positioning, and business model design. . Conducts interactive discovery process with structured questioning to gather all project brief elements before generating documents. ([TaylorHuston/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/TaylorHuston/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-toolkit/agents/brief-strategist.md) · ★13)

Use when the user has a draft brief, campaign concept, or project intent that needs to be turned into a structured input for further production. - **brief-synthesizer**: Merges the UX pattern research, personas and sitemap into one unified design brief, resolving conflicts between them. Stage 3 of the PRD pipeline — runs after all three parallel research agents finish.
